# Curve 0
def curve_0(t):
    nodes = [[820.0, 1.0], [821.0, 2.0], [822.0, 3.0], [823.0, 4.0]]
    x = ((1-t)**3)*nodes[0][0] + 3*((1-t)**2)*t*nodes[1][0] + 3*(1-t)*(t**2)*nodes[2][0] + (t**3)*nodes[3][0]
    y = ((1-t)**3)*nodes[0][1] + 3*((1-t)**2)*t*nodes[1][1] + 3*(1-t)*(t**2)*nodes[2][1] + (t**3)*nodes[3][1]
    return [x, y]

    return [x, y]
